<body style="padding: 20px 100px;">
<div>
<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">To SSML Teams</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Cc SSML &ndash; managed Fleet ( bcc)</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Good-day</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span></p>

<ol style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:13.35pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:4.65pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>A US Federal COFR is required for ships that call, or are likely to call, US ports. </span><span>&nbsp;</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The COFR is issued subject to there being in place , valid underlying ship&rsquo;s P&amp;I insurance for oil pollution, and a further top-up insurance purchased from a specialist insurer, each ship. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">We purchase this specialist cover through SIGCO . </span></p>

<ol start="2" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:13.35pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:4.65pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>SIGCO cover premium is offered in two options: </span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Full Trading cover : for ships </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">certain</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> to call US ports. 100% full premium is payable .</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Partial Service cover : for ships that </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">may </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">call US ports. A lower </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">deposit premium only, is payable. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Fully-paid</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> ships , are published by the USCG NPFC on its website , with their COFR and VIN no, respectively. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Part-paid ships are not shown. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">For a part-premium paid ship, if it is ordered to the US , SIGCO then upgrades the part deposit to full trading premium, to obtain the COFR . ( </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">see</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> item 6 below) </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="3" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:13.35pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:4.65pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>The US Federal COFR is issued for a 3-year period , but this is provided the underlying insurances as stated in item 1, get renewed. </span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The ship&rsquo;s P&amp;I and SIGCO insurances are however , issued with a 1-year validity only. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Therefore, it is important we ensure that the underlying insurances are kept valid. </span></p>

<ol start="4" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:13.35pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:4.65pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>&lsquo;Seaspan Ship Management Ltd&rsquo; is named as the &lsquo;applicant&rsquo; and the vessel &lsquo;operator&rsquo; for the COFR. </span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">This is for simplicity. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Note: the definition of &lsquo;operator&rsquo; under COFR is different from that in commercial circles. </span></p>

<ol start="5" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:12.74pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:5.26pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span style="font-size:11pt">The SIGCO insurance for your vessel </span><span style="font-size:11pt">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-size:11pt">COFR, has been renewed for one year from 01 July 2022, but only if the </span><span style="font-size:11pt">vsl</span><span style="font-size:11pt"> is trading (or likely) to the US - as follows :</span><span> </span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold; text-decoration:underline">Full Trading cover </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold; text-decoration:underline">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:115%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> ONE Parana</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:115%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> ONE Magdalena</span></p>

DMSDOC14352 | 2022 MARINE STANDARDS ROVING TEAM MEETING WITH INGENIUM | RF-5783 | v1.0 | DELE | ver A | RF | flag H |  | parent DMSDOC8315 | published 2026-02-10 19:50:21 | deleted 2026-02-10 19:50:21 | 
<html>
<head>
	<title></title>
	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/lite.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite__css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />
	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/opentip.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite_tt_css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />
</head>
<body style="padding: 20px 100px;">
<div>
<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:center; font-size:14pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold; background-color:#ffffff">MEETING POINTS OF DISCUSSION AT MUMBAI BETWEEN INGENIUM AND MARINE STANDARDS ROVING AUDITOR TEAM</span></p>

<ol style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="font-weight:bold; color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Steering test</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff"> - Guidelines provided in the procedures are ambiguous and interpreted in various ways by the ship staff. Recommend that a detailed extent of the test is specified for arrival so that vessels can follow the same in a uniform manner . The scope of the test could be in close similarity with the USCG requirements so that vessels are familiar with the process when arriving in US ports.</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="font-weight:bold; color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Main Engine Test</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">: Noted that some vessels are following the procedure, however for vessels calling ports with congested waters or vessels with time constraints to keep to the ETA , tend to test the engines only from one location. Recommend that either vessels test from all 3 areas outside confined waters or the procedure for testing from all 3 locations is relaxed giving the ship master&rsquo;s more leeway especially when in congested waters . Suggest that the test from local control be extended as testing from all 3 locations ( local control included ) is time consuming.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="background-color:#ffffff">The importance of the use of Radar equipment on sea stabilized mode to be emphasized with the ship staff mainly for navigation in open coastal waters and open sea.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="font-weight:bold; color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Glitches in One-Ocean Passage planning software</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff"> in </span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">the areas</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff"> such as </span></li>
</ol>

<ol style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="i">
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>During voyage planning, </span><span>software</span><span> does not suggest all the required ENC for the voyage. It only shows ENC which have valid permits. Selecting specific folio would </span><span>open up</span><span> all the ENCs. </span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Appropriate ENCS for the passage not included in the ENC list at times and</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">ENC list columns incomplete at times. The NTM to which the ENCs are corrected is not mentioned. Instead, </span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">a message</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff"> &quot; No updates collected&quot; is at times visible.</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Radio contacts and NAVTEX stations not evident / incorrect.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Tidal data not available for certain ports.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>Arrival and departure port information does not contain important details such as maximum dimensions permitted for the port and safe periods for transit. In the case of certain ports most of the port specific information is missing.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:3pt; margin-left:41pt; margin-bottom:3pt; padding-left:13pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>One Ocean to be contacted </span><span>in regard to</span><span> the instances when minimum depth and CATZOC values in the passage plan can be manually changed and these instructions to be passed to Masters.</span><span>&nbsp;</span></li>
</ol>

<ol start="5" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="1">
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="font-weight:bold; color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">T and P plotting</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">: Distance from the charted track where in T and P should be manually plotted to be clearly established in the ECDIS procedures.</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Plotting of T and P in lieu of solely activating the AIO to be done.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">Port specific information from Sailing directions and from other sources to be included in the passage plan.</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span><span style="color:#242424; background-color:#ffffff">&nbsp;</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>Procedure to be established on the required sections of the passage plan which are to be mandatorily included. There is no uniformity among the submitted plans. Many vessels submit passage plans with the AIO information, NAVAREA Warnings, MARPOL, Port specific information, Passage plan notes (1</span><span style="font-size:8pt; vertical-align:super">st</span><span>&nbsp;</span><span>page of the plan)</span><span>&nbsp;</span><span>and weather information sections of the plan missing.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>Masters to be reminded that when berthing times are changed, UKC calculations to be revisited and it is to be re-ascertained if company UKC requirements will be complied </span><span>with. In these circumstances an amendment to the passage plan is required to show continued compliance with company UKC requirements.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:32.11pt; margin-bottom:6pt; padding-left:3.89pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:12pt; background-color:#ffffff"><span>From the investigations conducted -a focus area which needs improvement is the Master pilot information exchange and a discussion of the berthing / unberthing maneuver. This will prevent over dependence on the pilot and ensure that the bridge team is aware of the maneuver and will be ready to challenge the pilot if/ when things go wrong.</span></li>
</ol>
</div>
</body>
</html>

DMSDOC18942 | SHIPBOARD MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE | SP-0099 | v2.0 | DELE | ver A | SP | flag H |  | parent DMSDOC8268 | published 2026-02-10 19:50:59 | deleted 2026-02-10 19:50:59 | Procedure Number Updated
<html>
<head>
	<title></title>
	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/lite.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite__css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />

<body class="SEASPAN Custom_dropdown" data-wsc-instance="true" style="padding: 20px 94.7619px;">
<div>
<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>
</div>

<p><span class="lovoy_header" style="background:#00475b;color:#ffffff;display:inline-block;font-family:Calibri;font-size:24px;font-weight:bold;padding:4px;width:100%;">Procedure: Shipboard Management Committee</span></p>

<h1 class="lovoy_h1" style="color:#002060;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;">Purpose</h1>

<p class="lovoy_normal">To provide guidance to Ship and Shore personnel on how to organize and run the Shipboard Management Committee</p>

<hr align="center" size="2" style="width:100%" />
<h1 class="lovoy_h1" style="color:#002060;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;">Scope</h1>

<p class="lovoy_normal">This applies to all ships managed by Seaspan Ship Management Ltd.</p>

<div align="center">
<hr align="center" size="2" style="width:100%" /></div>

<h1 class="lovoy_h1" style="color:#002060;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;">Responsibility</h1>

<p class="lovoy_normal">The Master is responsible to implement this procedure.</p>

<div align="center">
<hr align="center" size="2" style="width:100%" /></div>

<h1 class="lovoy_h1" style="color:#002060;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;">Procedure<span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></h1>

<p><span class="lovoy_note" style="background:#deeaf6;display:inline-block;font-family:Calibri;font-size:16px;padding:4px;width:100%;"><strong>Note: </strong>&nbsp;The Shipboard Management Committee consists of:<br />
Master - Chairman<br />
Chief Engineer - Member&nbsp;<br />
Chief Officer - Member<br />
Second Engineer - Member<br />
Electrical Officer - Member</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<h1 class="lovoy_h1" style="color:#002060;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;">Functionality</h1>

<p class="first_level" style="padding-left:0px;">Review the working of the &ldquo;health, safety and environment committee&rsquo; to ensure resources are well utilized.<br />
<span class="lovoy_caution" style="background:#f9d89a;border:1px solid #ff0000;display:inline-block;font-family:Calibri;font-size:16px;font-style:italic;padding:4px;width:100%;"><strong>Caution! </strong>Do not combine this meeting with any other meeting</span></p>

<p class="first_level" style="padding-left:0px;">Meet at least once every month with the following agenda:</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any outstanding action items since the last meeting?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">&nbsp;Any new incident(s) ?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any defects / PSC deficiencies ?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any Class and Statutory surveys / Conditions of class / Periodic service coming due ?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any commercial issues relating to charter party compliance?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any overdue audit / PSC CAR&rsquo;s?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any overdue drills? (List the drill / due date / planned date / reason&nbsp; overdue)</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any MLC related issues?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any critical issues affecting the vessels operations?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Any major jobs planned in the next 30 days?</p>

<p class="second_level" style="padding-left: 0px; margin-left: 40px;">Mess committee meeting</p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:5pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:justify; font-size:5pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p><span class="list_footer" style="background:#fffaf1;border-bottom:1px solid #000000;border-top:1px solid #000000;display:inline-block;font-family:Calibri;font-size:16px;text-align:center;width:100%;">Completed&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:54pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:130%; font-size:14pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<div style="-aw-headerfooter-type:footer-primary">
<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:center; font-size:7pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>
</div>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold">I CLAN &ndash; PORTAL FOR VDR PLAYBACK, NAVIGATION AUDITS AND ASSESSMENTS </span></p>

<ol style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:14.6pt; padding-left:3.4pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Scope of Service and functionality offered</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">1)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Easy play back using log in and credentials, web based portal through a secure internet connection.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">2)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">VDR data playback available as per</span></p>

<ol style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="a">
	<li style="margin-left:67.05pt; padding-left:4.95pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Equipment data captured in VDR</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:67.56pt; padding-left:4.44pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Audio (processed for improvement in sound quality)</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:66.43pt; padding-left:5.57pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>CCTV ( if available)</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:67.56pt; padding-left:4.44pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Parameter Graphs and dials</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:67.25pt; padding-left:4.75pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Analytics from observations made on checklist</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:65.13pt; padding-left:6.87pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Repository for all supporting documents</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:66.95pt; padding-left:5.05pt; font-family:Calibri; font-size:11pt"><span>Playback on maps, ENC from NOAA,&nbsp; with integration on pay per view with ENC tiles</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">3)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Easy play back for reviews and briefing</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">4)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">User access to ships, fleets as per company roles and responsibilities</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">5)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">On</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">-</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">line checklist for VDR audits with references to company SMS, and other industry references</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">6)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Online report viewing and creation.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">7)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Ability to Benchmark trends across companies if agreed to share information on finding frequencies as per the standard BPG checklist</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="2" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:14.12pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:3.88pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Terms and Conditions</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="3" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:13.95pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:4.05pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Period:</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> The service </span><span style="font-weight:normal">is contracted</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> for a period of 12 months</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="width:36pt; display:inline-block">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="4" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:15.08pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:2.92pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Data Processing:</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Upon receipt of the VDR data from the vessel, VDR information will </span><span style="font-weight:normal">be played</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> back using the maker software, and extraction of data will be done. Subject to VDR manufacturer software providing a raw data extract dump.</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-weight:bold">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="5" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:13.03pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:4.97pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Availability on Portal</span><span>:</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> </span><span style="font-weight:normal">VDR Data would </span><span style="font-weight:normal">be processed</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> into the portal and made available in 2 working days</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> from the time complete data is </span><span style="font-weight:normal">received and verified to be complete.</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Detailed analytics </span><span style="font-weight:normal">uploaded </span><span style="font-weight:normal">within 5 working days from time of commencing the audit.</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> </span><span style="font-weight:normal">(if audit services </span><span style="font-weight:normal">included in scope of services</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Ingenium)</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="6" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:12.91pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:5.09pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>Data Storage:</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Would </span><span style="font-weight:normal">be stored</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> on cloud server retained </span><span style="font-weight:normal">for the duration of the</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> AMC</span><span style="font-weight:normal">, for CCTV the same would be maintained for a period of 3 months.</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> All data shall </span><span style="font-weight:normal">be stored</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> in Secure Google Cloud and Azure environment. Such data will </span><span style="font-weight:normal">be governed</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> by the </span><span style="font-weight:normal">NDA and confidentiality agreement with the client.</span></li>
</ol>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt">&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol start="7" style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="A">
	<li style="margin-left:14.82pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:3.18pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>User Access: </span><span style="font-weight:normal">Access to Ingenium </span><span style="font-weight:normal">i</span><span style="font-weight:normal">-CLAN </span><span style="font-weight:normal">possible only by creation of users and assigning credentials, passwords, </span><span style="font-weight:normal">fleets</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> and specific vessels</span><span style="font-weight:normal">. Allocation can be by</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Fleets </span><span style="font-weight:normal">to enable easy viewing and of assigned ships</span></li>

	<li style="margin-left:15.37pt; line-height:12.9pt; padding-left:2.63pt; font-family:Segoe UI; font-size:10pt; font-weight:bold"><span>&nbsp;</span><span>Support:</span><span style="font-weight:normal"> Support </span><span style="font-weight:normal">for access to Ingenium Portal and data related queries will be through standard email support as per working hours, working days in India. (GMT+5.5) </span><span style="font-weight:normal"> </span></li>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:center; font-size:1pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>
</div>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:4.75pt; margin-bottom:0pt; widows:0; orphans:0; font-size:10pt"><img alt="" height="35" src="/DMSCDN.war/resources/DMSImage/IMPRT3145RF-053.docx.002.png" style="-aw-left-pos:0pt; -aw-rel-hpos:column; -aw-rel-vpos:paragraph; -aw-top-pos:0pt; -aw-wrap-type:inline" width="603" /></p>

<p style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; break-inside: avoid; break-after: avoid; line-height: 108%; font-size: 16pt;"><span style="font-weight:bold;"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Purpose</span></span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The purpose of this instruction is to show OneOcean users where to find the help menus for guidance operating the program. The help menus inside the application should be utilized before reaching out to IT &amp; OneOcean support.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">For guidance on maintaining ENCs, Paper Charts &amp; Publications, please see the following standard procedure: </span> <span style="font-family:Calibri;"><a href="#" onclick="DMS_rc([{name:'DMSPageId',value:'DMSDOC14013'}]); return false; ">SP-198 ENCs, PAPER CHARTS AND PUBLICATIONS</a></span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">If your problem cannot be solved through the help tab you may contact Seaspan IT &amp; OneOcean via:</span></p>

<ul style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="disc">
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; margin-bottom:6pt; line-height:108%; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Seaspan IT Support -</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; letter-spacing:-0.2pt; color:#0562c1"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; text-decoration:underline; color:#0562c1">support@seaspan.zendesk.com</span></li>
</ul>

<div align="center">
<hr align="center" size="2" style="width:100%" /></div>

<p style="margin-top: 4.45pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; break-inside: avoid; break-after: avoid; line-height: 108%; font-size: 16pt;"><span style="font-weight:bold;"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#001f5f">Instructions</span></span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The below set of screenshots will show you how access the menus in the OneOcean application</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">:</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:25.5pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-19.85pt; line-height:130%; font-size:14pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">1)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Click on the top right of the screen where the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">&ldquo;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">H</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">elp&rdquo;</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> tab is located.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:1.35pt; margin-left:31.5pt; margin-bottom:10pt; line-height:108%; font-size:14pt"><img alt="" height="351" src="/DMSCDN.war/resources/DMSImage/IMPRT3146RF-053.docx.003.jpeg" style="border:0.75pt solid #000000; -aw-left-pos:0pt; -aw-rel-hpos:column; -aw-rel-vpos:paragraph; -aw-top-pos:0pt; -aw-wrap-type:inline" width="497" /></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:25.5pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-19.85pt; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:130%; font-size:14pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">2)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">A dropdown list will appear </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">w</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">here you can choose what type of help you require.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:31.5pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-after:avoid; widows:0; orphans:0; font-size:11pt"><img alt="" height="310" src="/DMSCDN.war/resources/DMSImage/IMPRT3147RF-053.docx.004.jpeg" style="border:0.75pt solid #000000; -aw-left-pos:0pt; -aw-rel-hpos:column; -aw-rel-vpos:paragraph; -aw-top-pos:0pt; -aw-wrap-type:inline" width="438" /></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:31.7pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-after:avoid; widows:0; orphans:0; font-size:5pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:25.5pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-19.85pt; line-height:130%; font-size:14pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">3)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">For example, if you select </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold">&ldquo;user guide&rdquo; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">the below screen will appear </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">revealing</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> a digital version of the OneOcean user guide. You can click through the table of contents, or search for your query using the icons </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">located </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">at the top left of the window.</span></p>

DMSDOC12423 | ISO 14001: 2015 MANAGEMENT REVIEW | RF-3319 | v1.0 | DELE | ver A | RF | flag H |  | parent DMSDOC12128 | published 2026-02-18 19:54:15 | deleted 2026-02-18 19:54:15 |

	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/opentip.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite_tt_css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />

<body>
<div>
<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-indent:18pt; text-align:center; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; font-weight:bold">Seaspan Ship Management Ltd. &ndash; ISO 14001: 2015 Management Review</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:18pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-indent:36pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; font-weight:bold">Date conducted: </span><span style="font-family:Arial; font-weight:bold">1</span><span style="font-family:Arial; font-size:7.33pt; font-weight:bold; vertical-align:super">st</span><span style="font-family:Arial; font-weight:bold"> Dec </span><span style="font-family:Arial; font-weight:bold">2020</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:54pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">a)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">Status of previous actions from management reviews</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:72pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-36pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">i)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial">Observations reported during Office Internal Audit </span><span style="font-family:Arial">conducted in May 2020, </span><span style="font-family:Arial">were </span><span style="font-family:Arial">closed</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> within the stipulated time frame. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:72pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-36pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">ii)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial">The NS5 (Company Planned Maintenance and HSQE management platform) was superseded by a new FMS (Fleet Management Software). Though platform offers enhanced interface from previous version, </span><span style="font-family:Arial">the system capability to generate data for analysis of environmental impacts is still under study. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:54pt; margin-bottom:6pt; text-indent:-18pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">b)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">Changes in:</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">1.&nbsp; internal/external inputs, </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Environmental Aspects database is maintained as an active document which is updated for </span><span style="font-family:Arial">any new environmental risks.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">2. Interested parties, </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">SP-619 highligh</span><span style="font-family:Arial">ts</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> various interested parti</span><span style="font-family:Arial">es and their environmental relation to</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> Seaspan Ship Management Ltd.</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> The document was reviewed and content found apt.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">3. Significant aspects</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">With the IMO&rsquo;s focus (MEPC 75 outcome) on reduction of GHG, one of the significant area of </span><span style="font-family:Arial">development would be to strengthen vessel SEEMP. A joint work group (ESG Workgroup) has been formed </span><span style="font-family:Arial">with stakeholders representing </span><span style="font-family:Arial">key</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> departments.</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> The ESG workgroup is expected to define sustainability targets by the Q1/2021. The ISO 14K Targets and Objectives will be reviewed to align with the Company ESG targets.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">4. Risks and opportunities</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">F</span><span style="font-family:Arial">ormation of ESG workgrou</span><span style="font-family:Arial">p and drive towards sustainable development will offer ample opportunities for streamlining processes and focused environmental objectives. With ever evolving regulatory framework </span><span style="font-family:Arial">both international and regi</span><span style="font-family:Arial">onal,</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> it is important to keep track of the developments</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> to avoid risk of non-compliance. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">In addition to updates being available through Company DOCMAP, our vessels also have access to Regulatory information through </span><span style="font-family:Arial">third party software (Regs4Ships) and more recently the ability to introduce geo-fencing in the Enviro-manager will enable overlaying Company requirements </span><span style="font-family:Arial">in addition to regional and international regulations. </span><span style="font-family:Arial"> </span><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp; </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:54pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-indent:-18pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">c)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">The extent to which environmental objectives have been achieved</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">We have set up </span><span style="font-family:Arial">six</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> environmental targets</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> and all </span><span style="font-family:Arial">but one </span><span style="font-family:Arial">have been met till date</span><span style="font-family:Arial">.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><img alt="" src="/DMSCDN.war/resources/DMSImage/IMPRT1963ISO_14001_2015_Management_Review_Seaspan_Ship_Management_Ltd_Ver_5.docx.001.jpeg" style="width: 624px; height: 210px;margin-left:55px;" /></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:54pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-18pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">d)</span><span style="font:7pt Times New Roman">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">Seaspan Ship Management Ltd. environmental performance </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">1. Non-conformities, </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">NIL</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">2. Monitoring &amp; measuring results, </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Objectives and targets are monitored on a monthly basis and uploaded on DOCMAP. The latest update will be available on DOCMAP</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">There is a </span><span style="font-family:Arial">2</span><span style="font-family:Arial">-</span><span style="font-family:Arial">month period to work on the &lsquo;root cause&rsquo; and &lsquo;preventative corrective action&rsquo;. </span><span style="font-family:Arial">M</span><span style="font-family:Arial">onitored by Marine Standards.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">3. Fulfil</span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">ment of compliance obligations</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Less than 20% of all foreign flagged vessels that operate in US meet strict eligibility criteria for QUALSHIP-21 certification by USCG. This year 31 vessels from our fleet which called ports in USA, qualified and attained certification. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">MARPOL violations have not been a cause of detention in our fleet and no incident </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">4. Audit results</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Office </span><span style="font-family:Arial">Internal Audit: </span><span style="font-family:Arial">May 2020</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Non-Conformities:&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="font-family:Arial">NIL</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Observations: </span><span style="font-family:Arial">4 nos.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Noteworthy comments:</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> 1 no.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Recommendations: </span><span style="font-family:Arial">5 nos.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Shipboard Audits &ndash; 39 (Nov 2020) </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">The </span><span style="font-family:Arial">findings from shipboard audits </span><span style="font-family:Arial">are to be analyzed and presented during the annual management review.</span><br />
&nbsp;</p>

<p style="margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 8pt; line-height: 108%; font-size: 11pt;"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="width:17.66pt; display:inline-block">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">e)&nbsp; Adequacy of resources</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">There are adequate resources in all departments to meet the needs of the ISO 14001:2015 standard.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span style="width:20.72pt; display:inline-block">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">f)&nbsp; Relevant communication from interested parties including complaints</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Seas</span><span style="font-family:Arial">pan received an Honorable mention for 2020 Admiral William M. </span><span style="font-family:Arial">Benkart</span><span style="font-family:Arial">, Marine Environment Protection Award for Excellence from the United States Coast Guard. The </span><span style="font-family:Arial">Benkart</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> award was created to recognize outstanding achievements in marine environmental protection that go beyond mere compliance with industrial and regulatory standards. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">As an extension of our commitment towards our customers (Charterers) we continue to voluntarily participate in the ESI (Environment Ship Index) and gain rating improvements to assist Charterers. Other initiatives with Charterers include strategic discussions on achieving reduction in GHG and improved Energy Efficiency.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-indent:36pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial; background-color:#c0c0c0">g)&nbsp; Opportunities for continual improvement</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Company initiative to reduce plastic waste from ships </span><span style="font-family:Arial">is making good progress. With supply of water filtration units to vessels it is expected that purchase of drinking water in plastic bottles will reduce and so will corresponding plastic waste. It is intended to </span><span style="font-family:Arial">achieve at-least a 10% YoY reduction in volume of Plastic waste from ships. A dashboard to monitor progress of the </span><span style="font-family:Arial">plastic waste volumes being discharged to shore reception facilities is planned for development.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Efforts to reduce fuel oil sludge and effectively manage the waste oils are in progress. A working group has been formed to review and promulgate best management practices.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">Next annual review: Tentatively in Dec 202</span><span style="font-family:Arial">1</span><span style="font-family:Arial"> along with the &lsquo;SSML annual management review&rsquo;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Arial">&nbsp;</span></p>

DMSDOC16383 | DF - AUXILIARY ENGINE SCR FAMILIARIZATION | RF-5484 | v2.0 | DELE | ver A | RF | flag H |  | parent DMSDOC8210 | published 2026-02-18 23:29:06 | deleted 2026-02-18 23:29:06 | 
<html>
<head>
	<title></title>
	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/lite.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite__css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />
	<link href="https://seaspan.macksvm.com/DMS/javax.faces.resource/ckeditor/plugins/lite/css/opentip.css.xhtml?ln=primefaces-extensions&amp;v=6.1.1" id="__lite_tt_css__" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" />

<body style="padding: 20px 100px;">
<div>
<div>
<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:center; font-size:1pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>
</div>

<p style="margin:0pt 0.9pt; line-height:24pt; border:0.75pt solid #003351; padding-right:4pt; padding-left:4pt; padding-bottom:1pt; background-color:#00475b"><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">Familiarization:</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff"> DF Equipment &ndash; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">PaNox </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">Marine </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">Selective Catalytic Reduction</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff"> Unit </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">for</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff"> Himsen </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-size:18pt; font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff">8H35DF Engines</span></p>

<h1 style="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:16pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Introduction &amp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Concept</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">:</span></h1>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Selective Catalytic Reduction (</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">SCR) unit</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> is needed </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">in</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">order to</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> regulate the NOx contents of the exhaust gas emissions from the Diesel generator engines</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The SCR is put into use when the engines operate on MGO/</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">fossil fuel and mostly </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">in port</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">. SCR is not required when the engine operated on LNG. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><img alt="DiagramDescription automatically generated" height="565" src="/DMSCDN.war/resources/DMSImage/IMPRT18108RF-5484.docx.002.png" style="-aw-left-pos:-2.19pt; -aw-rel-hpos:column; -aw-rel-vpos:paragraph; -aw-top-pos:67.74pt; -aw-wrap-type:topbottom" width="477" /><br />
<span style="font-family:Calibri">Selective Catalytic Reduction (SCR) selectively removes the oxides of nitrogen (NOx) in the</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">exhaust gases </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">from stationery engines</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> in the presence of a catalyst using ammonia (NH3) as a reduction</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">agent. By chemical reaction, this system produces harmless, nitrogen (N2) and water vapor (H2O),</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">which are discharged into the ambient air. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp;</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The ammonia is injected and mixed with the flue gas</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">prior to entering the catalyst</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> react</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">or which is installed in the uptake</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">An overvi</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">ew of the installation is as shown in the picture </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">above</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">There is one unit installed </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">for each generator on board, so there are </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">four</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> units on board this vessel. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The operation is via a touch screen HMI and </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">in auto mode. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Once the start button is pressed, t</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">he system will automatically </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">start in a pre</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">-</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">programmed start up</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> sequence. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Main Components of one SCR unit are</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> as follows:</span></p>

<ul style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="disc">
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">SCR Reactor with Catalyst </span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Integrated Dosing Unit: this will fulfill needs of all </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">four</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> units</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">.</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Urea Injector</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Mixer</span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">SCR Control Panel</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">: T</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">his is</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> a centralized control for all 4 SCR</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">.</span></li>
</ul>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The quantity of urea injected is based on the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">following factors: </span></p>

<ul style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="disc">
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; line-height:108%; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Q</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">uantity of exhaust gas generated by the engine</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> at each load point</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">.</span></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin:0pt; padding-left:0pt" type="disc">
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; line-height:108%; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">T</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">he exhaust gas temperature </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">at that load. </span></li>
	<li style="margin-left:28.06pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; padding-left:7.94pt; font-family:serif; font-size:11pt; -aw-font-family:Symbol; -aw-font-weight:normal; -aw-number-format:"><span style="font-family:Calibri">T</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">he pressure loss over the entire process</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> at different loads. </span></li>
</ul>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">A Brief Description of the Components is as follows: </span></p>

<h2 style="margin-top:2pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:13pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; text-decoration:underline; color:#002060">SCR Reactor with Catalyst</span></h2>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The SCR catalyst is the key material of SCR system. The catalyst is composed of the various active materials for the De-NOx reaction. NOx in combustion flue gas reacts on the catalyst surface with</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">injected ammonia. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">The elements </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">are bundled with the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">stainless-steel</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> frame</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">. One frame is referred to as a Module</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">. This allows easy handling of the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">SCR </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">catalyst for</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">transportation and</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">installation in the reactor.</span></p>

<h2 style="margin-top:2pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:13pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; text-decoration:underline; color:#002060">Urea Dosing Unit</span></h2>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Th</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">e</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> Urea Dosing Unit is the system for feeding the ammonia at the upstream of SCR catalyst.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">The aqueous urea flow-is controlled by the demand signal at the PLC of the control panel. The PLC&rsquo;s demand signal is determined by the table of Engine&rsquo;s load signal vs. a required reagent flow rate. It is injected into the SCR inlet duct through the ammonia injection nozzle. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">Aqueous</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> urea is converted to ammonia vapor and HNCO (iso-cyanic acid) in exhaust gas duct after</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">engine before the catalyst. Mechanism of urea decomposition </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">is </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">described </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">in the next section.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Approximate urea </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">c</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">onsumption</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri">expected is listed in the table below: </span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">F</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">or 9H35</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">DF:</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">&nbsp; </span></p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width:422.8pt; margin-left:5.2pt; border:0.75pt solid #000000; border-collapse:collapse">
	<tbody>
		<tr style="height:16.5pt">
			<td style="width:110.8pt; border-right-style:solid; border-right-width:0.75pt; border-bottom-style:solid; border-bottom-width:0.75pt; vertical-align:middle; background-color:#ffff99">
			<p style="margin-top:3.1pt; margin-right:18.85pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-align:center; font-size:11pt"><a name="_Hlk108085453"><span style="font-family:Calibri">Load</span><span style="font-family:Calibri">:</span><span style="font-family:Calibri"> %</span></a></p>
			</td>
			<td style="width:61.35pt; border-right-style:solid; border-right-width:0.75pt; border-left-style:solid; border-left-width:0.75pt; border-bottom-style:solid; border-bottom-width:0.75pt; vertical-align:middle">
			<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-align:center; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">100</span></p>
			</td>
			<td style="width:61.35pt; border-right-style:solid; border-right-width:0.75pt; border-left-style:solid; border-left-width:0.75pt; border-bottom-style:solid; border-bottom-width:0.75pt; vertical-align:middle">
			<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; text-align:center; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri">75</span></p>
			</td>
			<td style="width:61.35pt; border-right-style:solid; border-right-width:0.75pt; border-left-style:solid; border-left-width:0.75pt; border-bottom-style:solid; border-bottom-width:0.75pt; vertical-align:middle">
