<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">&nbsp;</span></p>

<h1 style="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:16pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Operational </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Precautions:</span></h1>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">The NOx removal reactions within the catalyst depend on flue gas temperature. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">As a general </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">guideline, </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">the</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> exhaust gas temperature at the inlet to SCR is should be ideally maintained at 300</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">~350</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> C</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">, </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">where the c</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">atalyst </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">functions at nearly</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> optimal</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> level. Overall </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">operating range</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> is </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">255 </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">~450 </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">C.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-weight:bold; color:#171717"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">T</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">he SCR is located in the exhaust gas duct</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">near the EGB </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">boiler</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> which allows </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">the temperature to be maintained at the required level. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Further there is a Waste</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> Gate Valve provided at the generator</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. The waste gate is </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">actu</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">ated based on the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">parameters </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">programmed into the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Combustion module</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. The valve al</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">lows the exhaust gases to </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">bypass</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> the turbocharger, boosting the exhaust temperature and reducing the scavenge</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> pressure to the levels required for optimal operation</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> for the load on the generator at that point. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">The waste gate is profitably deployed when the generator is on</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> low load. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">The relationship between NOx removal efficiency and ammonia slip is shown below. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">(1) H2N-CO-NH2(Urea) + Heat &rarr; NH3 + HNCO (Iso-cyanic acid)</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-left:36pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">(2) HNCO + H2O &rarr; NH3 + CO2</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717">It is important to monitor the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717">Ammonia injection quantity to ensure that wastage to</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717"> due </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717">to </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717">any</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717"> malfunction</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717"> is detected quickly for corrective action</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; font-style:italic; color:#171717"> and slip is minimized</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">The more ammonia is injected (increase NH3/NOx molar ratio), the higher NOx removal efficiency is achieved. On the other hand, the more ammonia is injected, the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">quantity </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">of slip</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> ammonia</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> increases, which </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">is wasteful and make the ammonia slip </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">(</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Isocyanic Acid HNCO production increases) </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">go over the </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">regulatory</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> limit.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> There is no allowance for excess </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Ammonia</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> in liquid or gaseous form in the regulation</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:6pt; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">The soot blowing operation is critical </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">as it will keep the catalyst stack clean. Soo</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">t blowing is a</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">n automatically </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">timed operation and uses </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">compressed air</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. The </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">blowers are static type with no moving parts. </span></p>

<h1 style="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:16pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Maintenance </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Precautions:</span></h1>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Ensure engine is</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> stopped, switched off</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> and locked from starting or </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">connection to MSB</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Ensure starting interlock is </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">operational, Tag and lock off in place</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> for all fluids, </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">gases,</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> and engines. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Ventilation and enclosed space entry </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">permit</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> required</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Gas meter to be enabled or alternative method for </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">checking </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">urea concentration provided prior entry</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">.</span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Ammonium bisulfate (ABS) condensation can happen at low temperatures, depending on the sulfur content of the flue </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">gas. ABS</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">, however, can be removed once the flue gas temperature is raised above the ABS catalyst dew point. </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Alternatively</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> it may need </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">to be cleaned manually </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">and the surfaces may be </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">hot.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Maintenance plan to be procured from the Makers before </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">identifying further</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">risks</span></p>

<h1 style="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:16pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Criticality &amp; </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Redundancy:</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">&nbsp; </span></h1>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">There </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">is one SCR per </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">AE.</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> If one SCR is out of order</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">, three other generators can handle the power requirements till the SCR is repaired. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">IF two SCRs are out of order power requirements can still be met </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">on sea passage (To be confirmed). Port arrival bow</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717"> </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">thruster needs </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">can be met with two AE&rsquo;s and possible temporary switching off of reefers.</span></p>

<h1 style="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; page-break-inside:avoid; page-break-after:avoid; line-height:108%; font-size:16pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Recommended </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#002060">Parts:</span></h1>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; text-decoration:underline; color:#171717">Quantities to be decided </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; text-decoration:underline; color:#171717">once more information is available. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">NOX sensor </span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">outlet</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">. </span></p>

<p style="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:8pt; line-height:108%; font-size:11pt"><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">Injection nozzle</span><span style="font-family:Calibri; color:#171717">.</span></p>
